```stl
      vertex 280 40 0
      vertex 280 40 10
      vertex 300 40 10
    endloop
  endfacet
  facet normal 0 1 0
    outer loop
      vertex 280 40 0
      vertex 300 40 10
      vertex 300 40 0
    endloop
  endfacet
  facet normal 0 1 0
    outer loop
      vertex 40 90 0
      vertex 40 90 10
      vertex 50 90 10
    endloop
  endfacet
  facet normal 0 1 0
    outer loop
      vertex 40 90 0
      vertex 50 90 10
      vertex 50 90 0
    endloop
  endfacet
  facet normal 0 1 0
    outer loop
      vertex 610 90 0
      vertex 610 90 10
      vertex 620 90 10
    endloop
  endfacet
  facet normal 0 1 0
    outer loop
      vertex 610 90 0
      vertex 620 90 10
      vertex 620 90 0
    endloop
  endfacet
endsolid bemly-index
```

两个分支 README 的全部文字（main 80 行 + master 独有 21 行）都用「观致 8×8 像素字体」逐字栅格化成了方块：

[bemly-full.stl](./bemly-full.stl) — 112712 个三角形，3200 × 10890 × 10 mm，点击即可在 GitHub 自带的 3D 查看器里旋转浏览全文。
